<h1>Available models</h1>
<p>Pass any of these ids to <code>-m</code>, <code>--model</code>, or pick them in-session with <code>/model</code>. Run <code>cmd --list-models</code> to print the same list in your terminal - these tables are generated from the same model registry that backs <code>--list-models</code> and the <code>/model</code> picker, so the three always carry the same models. The default model currently is <strong><code>deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash</code></strong>.</p>
<p>Sections below are one per <strong>company that makes the models</strong>, A→Z, with each company&#x27;s models listed A→Z inside. (<code>--list-models</code> and the <code>/model</code> picker group by serving lane instead, which puts every open-weight model under one &quot;Open Source&quot; heading - the same models, filed differently.)</p>
<div class="my-6 overflow-hidden xrounded-2xl bg-background border-border border shadow-md dark:ring-6 dark:ring-[#3B3765]"><div class="not-prose"><div class="group px-[6px] pb-[6px] pt-[6px]"><div class="relative bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-black xrounded-xl border-none px-4 py-4 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-border transition-all duration-200 hover:ring-2"><div class="xrounded-sm absolute right-0 top-0 z-10 size-2 -translate-y-0.5 translate-x-0.5 border border-border bg-background sm:size-2.5"></div><div class="xrounded-sm absolute bottom-0 right-0 z-10 size-2 translate-x-0.5 translate-y-0.5 border border-border bg-background sm:size-2.5"></div><div class="max-h-[600px] overflow-auto scrollbar-hide"><div style="background-color:#fff;margin:0;padding:0;background:transparent;font-size:13px;line-height:1.5"><code style="white-space:pre;word-break:normal;overflow-wrap:normal"><span>cmd --model claude-sonnet-5                    </span><span class="token" style="color:var(--code-muted)"># interactive session on Sonnet 5</span><span>
</span><span>cmd -p </span><span class="token" style="color:var(--code-accent)">&quot;summarize this repo&quot;</span><span> --model gpt-5.5   </span><span class="token" style="color:var(--code-muted)"># one-shot on GPT-5.5</span><span>
</span><span>cmd --model kimi-k2.5                          </span><span class="token" style="color:var(--code-muted)"># short form: name after the &quot;/&quot;</span><span>
</span><span>cmd --list-models                              </span><span class="token" style="color:var(--code-muted)"># print every id you can pass</span><span>
</span></code></div></div><button type="button" class="group/button xmd:opacity-100 xrounded-full absolute right-4 top-3.5 overflow-hidden py-1 pl-2 pr-3 text-2xs font-medium opacity-0 backdrop-blur transition focus:opacity-100 group-hover:opacity-100 bg-white/5 hover:bg-white/7.5 dark:bg-white/2.5 dark:hover:bg-white/5"><span aria-hidden="false" class="pointer-events-none flex items-center gap-0.5 text-zinc-400 transition duration-300"><svg viewBox="0 0 20 20" aria-hidden="true" class="h-5 w-5 fill-zinc-500/20 stroke-zinc-500 transition-colors group-hover/button:stroke-zinc-400"><path stroke-width="0" d="M5.5 13.5v-5a2 2 0 0 1 2-2l.447-.894A2 2 0 0 1 9.737 4.5h.527a2 2 0 0 1 1.789 1.106l.447.894a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v5a2 2 0 0 1-2 2h-5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2Z"></path><path fill="none" stroke-linejoin="round" d="M12.5 6.5a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v5a2 2 0 0 1-2 2h-5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-5a2 2 0 0 1 2-2m5 0-.447-.894a2 2 0 0 0-1.79-1.106h-.527a2 2 0 0 0-1.789 1.106L7.5 6.5m5 0-1 1h-3l-1-1"></path></svg>Copy</span><span aria-hidden="true" class="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center text-[#fad000] transition duration-300 translate-y-1.5 opacity-0">Copied!</span></button></div></div></div></div>
<p><code>--model</code> matching is <strong>case-insensitive</strong> and accepts either the <strong>full id</strong> or just the <strong>name after the <code>/</code></strong>, so <code>moonshotai/Kimi-K2.5</code>, <code>moonshotai/kimi-k2.5</code>, and <code>kimi-k2.5</code> all resolve to the same model. An unknown id is <strong>rejected</strong> up front (pointing you at <code>cmd --list-models</code>) rather than failing later with a cryptic provider error. Each model id below links to its profile page; models that don&#x27;t have a dedicated page yet redirect to the <a href="https://commandcode.ai/models" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="text-burple-foreground">models overview</a>.</p>
<p>Looking for plans, credits, and per-token rates? See <a class="text-burple-foreground" href="/docs/resources/pricing-limits">Pricing &amp; Limits</a>.</p>
